# Corpus classification for the M1 gate

Generated by `scripts/corpus/classify.py`; rules in `review/corpus-classification.md` §3 and its
gate rules of 2026-10-08. Each entry is one deduplicated program, run on a release-built
`karac __mir-run` with a 60 s timeout.

- **Gate (the M1 slice): 10370 / 10543 = 98.4%** passing (a, b, c, auto-resolved e-spec); bar 95%.
- Slice without `lib:`/`io:` programs: 8354 / 8487 = 98.4%.
- Every classified non-deferred program (context): 16967 / 18099 = 93.7%.
- Bench variants (outside the gate): 286 / 1004 = 28.5%; timeouts 640.
- Legacy test programs (`src:legacy-tests`, M3 item 9, outside the gate): 5395 / 5411 = 99.7%; failing mir-gap 16.
- Deferred (class d): 751; outside the slice (`post-m1:*`): 1141.
- mir-llvm (`karac __mir-llvm-run`), measured at d42264fd85: 9036 / 9218 = 98.0% of the slice; 8995 pass on both backends. Every executed program: 10168 / 10808.
